In 1950 , February 21 marked the day of the world 's first-ever International Pancake Race in the city of Liberal in the US state of Kansas.Each year the communities in Liberal and also the English town of Olney in Buckinghamshire hold a 380-metre race to determine the fastest runner who can also flip a pancake at the same time.Typically the fastest runner completes the course in just under one minute . <p> http : **40;3889;TOOLONG ... The bizarre tradition is celebrated elsewhere as Mardi Gras , Pancake Day , Carnival or Shrove Tuesday and marks the day before Ash Wednesday - the first day of religious Lent , a 40-day fasting period in preparation for Easter . The legend behind the race in Olney also dates back to 1445 and recounts a woman who was making pancakes when church bells began ringing to announce a local church service . Carrying her frying pan and wearing an apron , she raced to arrive in time and in years afterward , others in the community joined her to win a prize - a " Kiss of Peace " from the verger , or bell ringer . <p> http : **40;3931;TOOLONG ... In years since , a friendly rivalry developed between the cities of Liberal and Olney as to who could complete the race the fastest . The contest , which continues today , requires runners to wear a traditional apron and scarf , carry a frying pan and toss a pancake at the beginning and ending of the race . <p> http : **40;3973;TOOLONG ... The annual event in both cities has become so popular , members of the local community line the streets in commemoration of the race and to cheer on those who take part . <p> http : **40;4015;TOOLONG ... In 1965 , February 21 was the day of African-American Muslim minister and human rights activist Malcolm X 's assassination.On that day , Malcolm X had been preparing to deliver a speech in front of the Organisation of Afro-American Unity in New York City 's Audubon Ballroom when someone in the 400-person audience yelled , " N----r ! Get your hand outta my pocket ! " <p> http : **40;4057;TOOLONG ... That single moment sparked minutes of chaos as Malcolm X 's bodyguards tried to stop the disturbance.In the scuffle , a man rushed forward and shot the civil rights figure once in the chest with a sawed-off shotgun and two other men charged the stage firing semi-automatic handguns.Malcolm X was pronounced dead at 3.30pm , shortly after arriving at Columbia Presbyterian Hospital. 188 The autopsy identified 21 gunshot wounds to the chest , left shoulder , arms and legs , including ten buckshot wounds from the initial shotgun blast . <p> http : **40;4099;TOOLONG ... Throughout his life , as well as after his death , Malcolm X has been heralded as one of the greatest and most influential African Americans in US history.His achievements were credited with raising the self-esteem of black Americans and for the spread of Islam in the black community in the United States.To this day , his life is commemorated after he was posthumously honoured with ' Malcolm X Day ' in the US , where he is commemorated in various countries worldwide <p> http : **40;4141;TOOLONG ... In 1995 , American businessman and a record-setting aviator , sailor , and adventurer Steve Fossett became the first-ever person to make a solo flight across the Pacific Ocean in a hot air balloon . <p> http : **40;4183;TOOLONG ... The voyage took Fossett just four days to complete and spanned more than 8690 kilometres.As his balloon descended back to Earth for his landing in Canada , a convoy of cars followed along with him as an entire audience of supporters rushed to congratulate him for his achievement . <p> http : **40;4225;TOOLONG ... Fossett later told media that on the first night of the epic voyage , a burner failed and his balloon began to drop toward the ocean . He then tried firing up the burner , but spilled petrol ignited and burned holes in the lower edges of the balloon.Despite that , the adventurer still managed to cross the Pacific in a feat that inspired his next giant move - to become the first-ever person to circumnavigate the world , non-stop in any kind of aircraft in 2002 , which he also achieved with a balloon . <p> http : **40;4267;TOOLONG ... On February 20 , 1913 the construction of Australia 's man-made national capital , Canberra , officially began with then-Minister for Home Affairs King O'Malley driving the first survey peg into the ground where the city now stands.The transformation of the rural area Canberra once was to the centre of Australian politics and diplomacy was sparked by fierce debate between Sydney and Melbourne as to where the capital should be located . <p> http : **40;4309;TOOLONG ... After years of debate , a compromise was finally reached : the new capital would be built in NSW , so long as it was at least 160km from Sydney.O'Malley spearheaded the design process of the city , overseeing a competition people were allowed to enter with ideas for how the capital should look once built . <p> http : **40;4351;TOOLONG ... Throughout the entire process , the site went unnamed and so , in 1912 , the government invited suggestions from the public to name the future city . Almost 750 names were suggested , but at midday on March 12 , 1913 , Lady Denman , the wife of then-Governor-General Lord Denman , announced that the city would be named " Canberra " at a ceremony at Kurrajong Hill . <p> http : **40;4393;TOOLONG ... In 2003 , February 20 was the day that marked a tragic nightclub fire at The Station venue in West Warwick in the US state of Rhode Island.In total , 100 people - including Great White guitarist Ty Longley - were killed in the inferno and 230 others injured . It is believed the fire was caused by fireworks that were set off by a tour manager for the band , Great White , which ignited foam in the walls and ceilings surrounding the stage . <p> http : **40;4435;TOOLONG ... The intense fire exploded within one minute and began to burn any flammable materials all around the stage , band and fans in attendance.Within six minutes , the entire venue had been cloaked in thick , black smoke that hindered people 's ability to escape the venue . <p> http : **40;4477;TOOLONG ... Many of the survivors of the accidental fire developed post traumatic stress disorder as a result of psychological trauma from the tragedy . The incident also became the fourth-deadliest at a nightclub in US history and sparked a national outpouring of grief . <p> http : **40;4519;TOOLONG ... In 1877 , Pyotr Ilyich Tchaikovsky 's ' Swan Lake ' ballet performance was premiered for the first time on February 20 by the Bolshoi Ballet at the Bolshoi Theatre in Moscow.The performance was not well-received because most critics considered the piece to be far too complicated for ballet.The plotline , initially told in in two acts , was created from Russian and German folk tales that tell the story of Odette , a princess turned into a swan by an evil sorcerer 's curse . <p> http : **40;4561;TOOLONG ... In 1935 , February 20 was also the day Danish-Norwegian explorer Caroline Mikkelsen became the first woman to set foot on Antarctica.In celebration of the feat , Mikkelsen was photographed raising the Norwegian flag.Despite the achievement , she did not publicly speak about her voyage until 60 years after the landing . <p> http : **40;4603;TOOLONG ... On February 20 , 1998 , 15-year-old Tara Lipinski won the gold medal in women 's figure skating at the Japanese Olympic Winter Games in Nagano and became the youngest-ever gold medalist in her sport.After almost 10 years of performing , Lipinski and her teammate Michelle Kwan entered the 1998 Games as strong favourites for the gold medal . That season , Lipinski had been defeated twice by Kwan - who was considered the more artistic skater of the two . Despite that , Lipinski 's Olympic show was ultimately considered more technically difficult , winning her the Gold . <p> http : **40;4645;TOOLONG ... In 1942 , Darwin turned into flames and thick , black plumes of smoke in what was the largest single attack ever mounted by a foreign power on Australia.On February 19 , 242 Japanese aircraft attacked the Northern Territory 's capital city in two separate raids , sunk ships in its harbour and bombed two airfields in an attempt to prevent the Allies from using them as bases to stop the invasion of Timor and Java during World War II . <p> http : **40;4687;TOOLONG ... Ahead of the first raid on Darwin , Father McGrath of the Sacred Heart mission on Bathurst Island sent a message at 9.35am , that reached the Australian Defence Force two minutes later , that a large number of aircraft were flying overhead and proceeding southward . Despite that , no general alarm was raised by the ADF until about 10am due to an incorrect interpretation of the type of planes that were heading for the city . <p> http : **40;4729;TOOLONG ... At 9.58am , the bombs began to rain down on ships in Darwin 's harbour for 30 minutes straight.Darwin was lightly defended relative to the size of the attack , and the Japanese forces caused heavy losses for Australians and the Allies.Further to that , the flow-on effect of fear stemming from the bombings after the raid meant that more than half of Darwin 's population left the area permanently , before or immediately after the attack . <p> http : **40;4771;TOOLONG ... The two Japanese air raids on February 19 were the first - and largest - of more than 100 air raids that were waged against Australia between 1942 and 1943 . In the first two alone , 236 people were killed , up to 400 injured , 30 aircraft were destroyed and 11 ships sunk . <p> http : **40;4813;TOOLONG ... In 1945 , February 19 marked the day one of the United States ' most famous-ever wartime photographs was snapped at the culmination of the Battle of Iwo Jima in World War II . ' Raising the Flag on Iwo Jima ' shows six US Marines raising their nation 's flag atop Mount Suribachi during a month-long battle that ultimately saw more than 26,000 people killed . <p> http : **40;4855;TOOLONG ... The battle was first sparked by an American invasion o f Iwo Jima with the goal of capturing the entire island to provide a future area for attacks on the Japanese main islands.The intense battle that then commenced saw some of the fiercest and bloodiest fighting of the Pacific War of World War II . <p> http : **40;4897;TOOLONG ... Despite the US winning the battle , Japanese combat deaths - which numbered three times the number of American deaths - were ultimately exceeded by the Americans ' total number of casualties.That point became controversial in history as the victory gave the US very little strategic benefit because Iwo Jima was later considered useless to both the Army and Navy for a future base . <p> http : **40;4939;TOOLONG ... In 1913 , February 19 was the day Mexican politician Pedro Lascur ? in Paredes became the world 's shortest-ever reigning President during a coup that saw him instated and then resign within a 45-minute period.His leadership began following the overthrow of then-President Francisco I. Madero by General Victoriano Huerta - who installed Lascur ? in , only to have him resign in order to satisfy the Mexican constitutional law that relied on a chain of command should a President be ousted . <p> http : **40;4981;TOOLONG ... The Chicago Seven trial symbolised the generational fury at the Vietnam War in the US . The activists were charged with conspiring to incite a riot after marches and rock gigs turned into violent clashes with police . Anti-war activist Jerry Rubin speaks with ( from left ) Rennie Davis , Rubin , Abbie Hoffman . Back row , from left : Lee Weiner , Bob Lamb and Thomas Hayden . <p> http : **40;5023;TOOLONG ... The trial was a chaotic shambles that lasted more than four months and sparked nearly daily protests on the streets . Abbie Hoffman was among the Chicago Seven . The well-known counter culture activist was immortalised in this scene of Forrest Gump . <p> http : **40;5065;TOOLONG ... Elm Farm Ollie was the first cow to fly in a plane when the beast was hoisted aboard on February 18 , 1930 . The experiment allowed scientists to observe the effects of flight on animals . <p> http : **40;5107;TOOLONG ... On the same day as Elm Farm Ollie 's maiden flight , Clyde Tombaugh was studying photographs and discovered Pluto . <p> http : **40;5149;TOOLONG ... Italian philosopher and mathematician Giordano Bruno was tried for heresy by the Roman Inquisition over his denial of core Catholic doctrines . He spoke against its ministers and regularly talked of reincarnation and the plurality of worlds . He was burned at the stake with an iron pike through his tongue to ensure he did n't spread any more wickedness . <p> http : **40;5191;TOOLONG ... Kosovo declared independence from Serbia on February 17 , 2008 . At least 150,000 Serbs gathered in Central Belgrade some days later to protest the declaration , which was triggered followed the breakdown of UN-mediated talks . <p> http : **40;5233;TOOLONG ... Brisbane-based budget airline Air Australia went into administration on February 17 , 2012 , leaving about 4000 passengers stranded in Indonesia , Phuket and Hawaii on round-trip tickets . <p> http : **40;5275;TOOLONG ... US President Richard Nixon departed the US for an unprecedented diplomatic trip to China on February 17 , 1972 . The seven-day official visit helped build bridges with mainland China , following a period of diplomatic isolation . Nixon is pictured here banqueting with Chinese Premier Chou En-lai . <p> http : **40;5317;TOOLONG ... Heavy rain and a minor earthquake triggered a huge mudslide in Southern Leyte , Philippines , killing 1126 , on February 17 , 2006 . Hundreds of people were left trapped under wet mud up to 30 metres thick . <p> http : **40;5359;TOOLONG ... NASA 's Voyager 1 spacecraft passed the Pioneer 10 craft , becoming the most distant manmade object from Earth in space , on February 17 , 1998 . The Voyager , launched in 1977 to pass Jupiter and Saturn , reached a distance of 69.4 AU from the Sun , later crossing into interstellar space in August 2012 . <p> http : **40;5401;TOOLONG ... China invaded Vietnam , sparking a brief border war known as the Sino-Vietnamese War , on February 17 , 1979 . China 's goal was to dissuade Vietnam from continued occupation of Cambodia . In this picture , Vietnamese families flee the provincial capital of Lang Son , where some of the heaviest fighting occurred . <p> http : **40;5443;TOOLONG ... China succeeded in capturing several of Vietnam 's border cities before withdrawing . Retreating invaders blasted many houses in residential areas of the provincial town of Lao Cai , as well as public building and local industries . <p> http : **40;5485;TOOLONG ... Tens of millions of smokers were granted the right to launch a lawsuit against the US tobacco industry , claiming that the industry designed cigarettes to be addictive , on February 17 , 1995 . The lawsuit was thrown out on appeal the following year , saving the industry billions in claims . <p> http : **40;5527;TOOLONG ... More than 180 fires fanned by winds of up to 110km/h swept across Victoria and South Australia on February 16 , 1983 , killing 75 and destroying up to 3700 buildings . Seen here are the remains of a school at Mount Macedon , Victoria . <p> http : **40;5569;TOOLONG ... The Ash Wednesday bushfires were named the deadliest in Australian history , until 2009 's Black Saturday bushfires . Pictured here is the devastation wrought in Cockatoo , Victoria . <p> http : **40;5611;TOOLONG ... Livestock losses were in the hundreds of thousands , and insurance payouts cost a total of $1.3 billion , according to adjusted 2007 estimates . <p> Ukrainian-American John Demjanjuk faced trial for war crimes and crimes against humanity committed while he worked as a guard at Nazi extermination camps during WWII , on February 16 , 1987 . Years later , Demjanjuk , then aged 91 , was convicted as an accessory to the murder of 27,900 Jews and sentenced to five years in prison , having already served two . <p> http : **40;5653;TOOLONG ... Dutch astronomer Gerard Kuiper discovered Miranda , the smallest and innermost of Uranus ' five moons , on February 16 , 1948 . It was later photographed by NASA . <p> http : **40;5695;TOOLONG ... Ethiopia 's late Emperor Haile Selassie 's remains were found buried beneath the office of his successor , dictator and President Mengistu Haile Mariam , on February 16 , 1992 . Selassie , 83 , was overthrown in a military coup that brought an end to a monarchy that had lasted for 3000 years . <p> http : **40;5779;TOOLONG ... Serial killer Jeffrey Dahmer , dubbed the " Milwaukee Cannibal " and " Milwaukee Monster " , was found sane and guilty of killing 15 young men and boys on February 15 , 1992 . Dahmer committed acts of necrophilia and cannibalism and even preserved the body parts of his victims . He was beaten to death by another inmate in prison in 1994 . <p> http : **40;5821;TOOLONG ... Bodies consigned to the Tri-State Crematory in La Fayette , Georgia for cremation were found buried in and around the site , on February 15 , 2002 . Marsh provided families of those sent to the crematory with concrete dust instead of ash . He was sentenced on 787 counts and served 12 years in jail . <p> http : **40;5905;TOOLONG ... Philippines President Ferdinand Marcos is declared the winner of an allegedly rigged presidential election on February 15 , 1986 . The results spark popular demonstrations that come to be known as the People Power Revolution , which resulted in the end of Marcos ' 21-year presidential rule and a return to democracy . <p> http : **40;6115;TOOLONG ... The UK and Ireland decimalized the existing currency of pounds , shillings and pence on February 15 , 1971 . " Decimal Day " brought to an end a system that first began in the 7th century . Pictured here are images of Britain 's then-new coinage . <p> http : **40;6157;TOOLONG ... The Soviet Union completely withdrew its troops from Afghanistan on February 15 , 1989 . TODAY IN HISTORY : February 14 - In 1929 , Valentine 's Day turned fatal when seven members and associates of Chicago 's North Side Gang were violently killed in a brutal massacre thought to have been masterminded by notorious underworld criminal Al Capone.The violent gangland shooting is believed to have resulted from an ongoing struggle to control organised crime in Chicago during the USA 's Prohibition-era between the Irish North Siders , headed by George ' Bugs ' Moran , and their Italian South Side rivals led by notorious criminal Al Capone.Within days of the killings , Capone received a summons to testify before a Chicago grand jury on charges of Prohibition violations , but he claimed to be too unwell to attend . <p> http : **40;6367;TOOLONG ... To this day , the bullet holes in the wall in front of which the seven men were lined up and executed can still be seen.For many years , the bricks were displayed in museum exhibitions after they were sold off.The remainder are now owned by the Mob Museum in Las Vegas . <p> http : **40;6409;TOOLONG ... In a peculiar aspect of the massacre , the only survivors found inside the warehouse after the shootings were a pet dog named " Highball " and mobster Frank Gusenberg -- despite 14 bullet wounds . <p> http : **40;6451;TOOLONG ... In 2003 , February 14 was the day Dolly , a six-year-old domestic sheep - and the world 's first-ever mammal cloned from an adult cell - was euthanised because she had a progressive lung disease and severe arthritis.Dolly left behind an historic , scientific legacy in that , after her cloning was successfully demonstrated , many other large mammals were cloned.As of July 2016 , four identical clones of Dolly - named Daisy , Debbie , Dianna , and Denise - were all alive and healthy at nine years old . <p> http : **40;6493;TOOLONG ... In 2005 , global video-sharing platform YouTube was introduced to the world by PayPal employees and university students Steve Chen , Chad Hurley , and Jawed Karim.Karim has said the inspiration for YouTube first came from Janet Jackson 's infamous 2004 Super Bowl incident , when her breast was exposed during her performance , and also the 2004 Indian Ocean tsunami . He said he came up with the idea after not easily locating video clips of either event online . <p> http : **40;6535;TOOLONG ... After a US$1.65 billion Google buyout , the website has since become one of the world 's largest social media-sharing websites and boasts roughly 500 hours of video being uploaded to the website every minute.Based on reported quarterly advertising revenue , the platform is estimated to have US$15 billion in annual revenues . <p> http : **40;6577;TOOLONG ... In 2008 , then-Australian Prime Minister Kevin Rudd made political history in delivering the nation 's first Apology to the Stolen Generations.The address has since been heralded as one of the defining moments in modern Australian history.Following a recommendation from The National Inquiry into the Separation of Aboriginal Children from their Families , Mr Rudd 's government committed itself to delivering the apology , highlighting the suffering of Indigenous families under the Commonwealth , state and territory Aboriginal protection and welfare laws and policies . <p> http : **40;6619;TOOLONG ... Across the nation , members of the Indigenous Australian population stopped to watch Mr Rudd 's address , with emotional scenes of jubilation and relief broadcast nationally.Members of the Stolen Generations were also invited to hear the Apology first-hand in the gallery of The House of Representatives chamber at Parliament House in Canberra . <p> http : **40;6661;TOOLONG ... Crowds of people across Australia - including thousands of children in schools in every state - watched the Apology on big screens in their own cities and towns.As the Prime Minister spoke of the wrongs governments had inflicted on Indigenous peoples , waves of tears , relief and applause flowed from inside the House of Representatives to the streets of Indigenous communities . <p> http : **40;6703;TOOLONG ... Following the address , Mr Rudd was embraced by members of the Indigenous community - including Stlen Generations survivor Lorna Fejo , who he mentioned in his speech.To this day , the historic moment is marked and commemorated as part of Australia 's annual " Sorry Day " in a gesture toward Indigenous communities . <p> http : **40;6745;TOOLONG ... In 1978 , February 13 was the day of the fatal Sydney Hilton Hotel bombing in the city 's CBD.At the time the hotel had been hosting the first Commonwealth Heads of Government Regional Meeting - a regional meeting of the heads of government from across the Commonwealth of Nations . <p> http : **40;6787;TOOLONG ... After the bombing , it was revealed that a device had been planted in a rubbish bin and detonated when it was emptied into a garbage truck.That explosion killed two garbage men - Alec Carter and William Favell - as well as the police officer guarding the entrance to the hotel , Paul Burmistriw . The bombing also injured 11 other people , however the 12 foreign leadres at the hotel at the time were unscathed . <p> http : **40;6829;TOOLONG ... In 1989 - 11 years after the bombing - Evan Pederick confessed to planting the device , and was convicted of murder and sentenced to 20 years in jail . He alleged the attack was an attempt to assassinate the then prime minister of India , Morarji Desai . <p> http : **40;6871;TOOLONG ... In 2000 , February 13 was the day the final-ever PEANUTS comic strip was released - just hours after the death of its writer and creator , Charles M. Schulz.The strip had been planned ahead of his death and presented a letter from the creator to the series ' fans , using its popular characters . <p> http : **40;6913;TOOLONG ... In 1993 , February 12 was the day that marked the tragic death of two-year-old British boy James Bulger after being abducted , tortured and killed by two 10-year-old boys who led him away from his mother in a supermarket.Over a period of months , the high-profile case prompted widespread public debate about how to handle young criminals during court sentencing and custodial release . <p> http : **40;6955;TOOLONG ... CCTV vision from the day of Bulger 's abduction shows his two attackers - Robert Thompson and Jon Venables in the New Strand Shopping Centre in the suburb of Bootle , observing children and apparently selecting a target.The two young boys had been playing truant from school , which they did regularly.After spotting the two-year-old Bulger , Thompson ( centre picture ) and Venables ( centre-right holding Bulger ) took him by the hand in a moment that his mother had been distracted and led him out of the shopping centre . <p> http : **40;6997;TOOLONG ... Over a period of hours , the two attackers tortured Bulger , physically assaulting him and beating him so badly pathologists were unable to pinpoint which injury had killed the toddler.The two boys left Bulger weighed down on a train line and his body was later cut in two by a passing carriage before his mutilated body was found two days after his abduction.Venables and Thompson were ultimately found guilty on 24 November 1993 , making them the youngest convicted murderers in modern British history . <p> http : **40;7039;TOOLONG ... February 12 was also the day in 1994 that Edvard Munch 's famous painting , ' The Scream ' , was stolen from the National Art Museum in Oslo , Norway.The thieves demanded a $1 million ransom for its return , causing a sting operation to be launched by Norwegian authorities with the assistance of British law enforcement . <p> http : **40;7081;TOOLONG ... The painting was recovered undamaged just two months later , and four perpetrators were imprisoned.Security camera footage showed two men climbing a ladder during the heist , smashing a gallery window , cutting ' The Scream ' off the wall with wire cutters and subsequently fleeing with the painting . The heist took a mere 50 seconds and the thieves left a postcard behind in the gallery , saying " Thanks for the poor security . " The group was said to have been led by P ? l Enger , who was sentenced to six years and three months in prison . <p> http : **40;7123;TOOLONG ... In 1912 , February 12 was the day the last-ever Emperor of China abdicated his throne after the Xinhai Revolution.Known as Puyi , or the Xuantong Emperor , the boy was aged just six years old when he was forced to relinquish his rule . <p> http : **40;7165;TOOLONG ... While Puyi was briefly restored to the throne as emperor by the warlord Zhang Xun from July 1 to 12 in 1917 , the boy leader was ultimately expelled from the Forbidden City in 1924 , abolishing his imperial title and privileges and reducing him to a private citizen of the Republic of China.From there , he moved to the country 's north-east and led a relatively vibrant political life , becoming the emperor of the Japanese puppet state of Manchukuo in 1932 , imprisoned as a war criminal after the establishment of the People 's Republic of China in 1949 and dying of kidney cancer and heart disease on 17 October 1967 , aged 61 . <p> http : **40;7207;TOOLONG ... In 2001 , February 11 was the day 20-year-old Dutch student Jan de Wit unleashed a computer worm onto the Internet designed to rapidly spread among users.Working under the alias ' OnTheFly ' , de Wit created and distributed the malicious program using the image of famous Russian tennis star Anna Kournikova and tricking millions of people and email servers around the world . <p> http : **40;7249;TOOLONG ... After de Wit sent the virus out via an online news group , the program arrived to its victims in an email with the subject line " Here you have , ; 0 ) " and an attached file called **26;7291;TOOLONG email itself never presented an actual image of Kournikova , but when people attempted to open the image file a virus program was launched that forwarded itself to everybody in their address books . <p> http : **40;7319;TOOLONG ... De Wit ultimately himself in to authorities in the town of Sneek after he had realised the rapid spread of the virus.In a letter posted online , he admitted to creating the worm and using Kournikova 's beauty to trick victims , also saying his motivations was to see whether the IT community had learned to improve their cyber security.The 20-year-old was eventually sentenced to just 150 hours of community service . <p> http : **40;7361;TOOLONG ... In 1990 , February 11 marked the historic day South African anti-apartheid revolutionary and political leader Nelson Mandela was finally released from prison after serving 27 years for conspiring to overthrow the state.Holding the hand of his wife , Winnie , in front of amassed crowds and the press the event was broadcast live across the world . <p> http : **40;7403;TOOLONG ... Among the crowds of ultra-passionate supporters , Mandela was driven to Cape Town 's City Hall , where he gave a powerful speech declaring his continued commitment to peace and reconciliation with the white minority amid the apartheid.During that address , he also expressed hope that the South African government would agree to negotiations , so that ' there may no longer be the need for the armed struggle ' , and insisted that his main focus was to bring peace to the black majority . <p> http : **40;7445;TOOLONG ... In 2012 , one of the world 's most iconic singers and actresses , Whitney Houston , was found unconscious and later died in Suite 434 at the Beverly Hilton Hotel.A toxicology report later revealed the star had died by drowning and had a cocktail of drugs in her body at the time she passed away . <p> http : **40;7487;TOOLONG ... Houston 's death sparked an intense global outpouring of grief among her fans and celebrities who mourned her loss for months.Within a week of her death , an invitation-only memorial service was held in Newark , New Jersey , attended by high-profile stars , friends and family.The service was scheduled for two hours , but lasted four . <p> http : **40;7529;TOOLONG ... In 1990 , February 11 was also the day that then-undefeated , undisputed boxing heavyweight champion Mike Tyson lost his first bout to his extremely underrated opponent Buster Douglas , who had entered the match at odds of 42-1 of winning at all and knocked him out.In a famous scene during the 10th round , Tyson fumbled for his mouthpiece in the ring before sticking one end in his mouth with the other end hanging out.The champion tried to continue fighting after suffering a barrage of punches , but he was counted out . Buster Douglas then became the new undisputed heavyweight champion and the fight became one of the biggest upsets in boxing history . <p> http : **40;7571;TOOLONG ... NASA spacecraft Galileo conducted a successful gravitational assist flyby of Venus , on February 9 , 1990 . This is a photo of Venus taken by the craft days later . On February 7 , 1882 , John " The Boston Strong Boy " Sullivan became the last bare-knuckle boxing champion after knocking out famed contender Patrick " Paddy " Ryan in a nine-round thriller in Mississippi . <p> http : **40;8453;TOOLONG ... In the late 19th century , the brutality of bare-knuckle boxing -- and the realities of 80 + round fights -- caused public opinion to turn on the sport , forcing competitors into illegal backroom prize fights . <p> http : **40;8495;TOOLONG ... Because boxers did not wear gloves , cuts and broken bones were commonplace . After the Sullivan versus Ryan bout , spectators described both men as having " the appearance of painted savages " . <p> http : **40;8537;TOOLONG ... Although the fight seemed " only " to last nine rounds , boxers in the late 19th century fought under London Prize Ring Rules , which meant a round only ended when one or both boxers were put down , which bizarrely included wrestling takedowns . <p> http : **40;8579;TOOLONG ... The Black Saturday bushfires killed a total of 173 people in a disaster so large , the Victorian Coroner compared the death toll to an event similar to the London bombings in 2005 . <p> http : **40;8621;TOOLONG ... More than 3500 structures , including 2029 homes , were also lost in the disaster - leaving many people homeless . <p> http : **40;8663;TOOLONG ... It was later estimated the amount of energy released during the firestorm in the Kinglake-Marysville area was equivalent to the energy that would be released by 1500 Hiroshima-sized atomic bombs . <p> http : **40;8705;TOOLONG ... In 1845 , February 7 was the day the unique , priceless and irreplaceable Portland Vase - which had been exhibited in the UK 's British Museum in London - was shattered into ore than 80 pieces.The glass Roman vase , measuring 24.5cm high , was discovered in a funerary monument in Rome in the 16th century and thought to have dated back to the first Century BC . <p> http : **40;8747;TOOLONG ... It was when Irish visitor William Lloyd attended the museum after a string of days of alcohol consumption that the priceless artefact was destroyed when he drunkenly hurled a stone sculpture through the vase 's glass case.Lloyd was arrested and later appeared in court charged with causing wilful damage . Despite that , the law under which he was being prosecuted applied only to the destruction of objects worth no more than five pounds . As a result , he was convicted and was fined just three pounds . <p> http : **40;8789;TOOLONG ... In 1940 , Walt Disney premiered his second-ever animated feature film , ' Pinocchio ' - three years after ' Snow White and the Seven Dwarfs ' was first shown.Based on the Italian children 's novel ' The Adventures of Pinocchio ' by Carlo Collodi , the film was groundbreaking for effects animation , in giving realistic movement to vehicles , machinery , rain , lightning , smoke , shadows and water . <p> http : **40;8831;TOOLONG ... Although it was the first animated feature to win an Academy Award , ' Pinocchio ' was initially a box office disaster , due to World War II.After the conclusion of the war , however , Disney 's adaptation recovered a profit and is - to this day - considered one of the greatest animated films ever made . <p> http : **40;8873;TOOLONG ... On February 6 , 1938 a crowd of 35,000 people had flocked to Australia 's most famous beach when tragedy struck and Bondi 's lifesavers were left to handle a situation so dire it has forever been remembered as ' Black Sunday ' . In a freak event of nature , the waves at Bondi Beach that day began to roll in so frequently that a backwash so large was created that it began to sweep hundreds of unaware swimmers out to sea . <p> http : **40;8915;TOOLONG ... " There was instant panic . Men , women and children fighting for their lives , " the Bondi Surf Life Savers Club has said about the incident . " It was mass hysteria at its worst . In the frenzy they shouted , screamed , cried , begged and prayed . They grabbed , clawed and fought . " On the beach there was a stunned but short-lived silence . " <p> http : **40;8957;TOOLONG ... Lifesavers spread along the beach who were preparing for local swim competitions leaped into action and began to rescue and resuscitate as many swimmers as they could.Bondi 's surf clubhouse was instantly turned into a makeshift emergency ward where drowning swimmers were hurriedly taken , while more than 30 resuscitations happened on the beach itself.After 30 long minutes , 250 swimmers had needed help , 150 were rescued unharmed , 60 suffered immersion , 35 were rescued unconscious and revived and five people were tragically killed . <p> http : **40;8999;TOOLONG ... In 1971 , February 6 was the day Apollo 14 NASA astronaut Alan Shepard made history by becoming the first human to ever hit a golf ball from the surface of the moon.Shepard brought a six iron golf club head with him during NASA 's third mission to the moon , which he attached to the handle of a lunar excavation tool and used to hit two golf balls.He famously claimed that the second ball went " miles and miles and miles " in the low lunar gravity , but it was later estimated the distance was between 180 and 370 metres . <p> http : **40;9041;TOOLONG ... February 6 also marked the day of the Northeastern United States blizzard of 1978 that caused catastrophic scenes across the New England , New Jersey and New York regions of the country 's east.During the freak weather event , Boston received a record-breaking 69cm of snow , while Providence got 70 cm and Atlantic City broke an all-time storm accumulation with 51 cm . <p> http : **40;9083;TOOLONG ... The storm was so intense that it completely buried cars , leaving highways stuck at a standstill and suburban streets inundated.The event also killed about 100 people in the north-east and injured about 4500 , while causing more than US$520 million in damage . <p> http : **40;9125;TOOLONG ... In 1994 - 31 years after the tragic and racially-fuelled shooting death of US civil rights leader and activist Medgar Evers - his family finally received justice for his killing.On February 5 , white supremacist and Ku Klux Klansman Byron De La Beckwith was finally convicted of his murder . <p> http : **40;9167;TOOLONG ... At the time of his first trials in 1964 , all-white juries were left deadlocked on De La Beckwith 's guilt and failed to reach a verdict - therefore laving his fate undecided for three decades to come.De La Beckwith was accused of shooting Evers in the back with a rifle after he returned to his Mississippi home from an NAACP lawyers meeting . <p> http : **40;9209;TOOLONG ... During multiple investigations of the high-profile death , it was revealed that Evers had been shot through the heart with this Enfield 1917 rifle.The murder came at an era in US history that was incredibly tense and Ever 's death sparked widespread protests and street marches that mounted pressure on investigators and the court process itself . <p> http : **40;9251;TOOLONG ... Ever 's wife , Myrlie , was the first person to find the civil rights activist after the shooting and rushed him to hospital , where he died after just 50 minutes.Following the failure of De La Beckwith 's first two trials , she never gave up hope in attempting to have the case re-trialled and - after 30 years - the white supremacist was convicted based on new evidence and the exhumation of Evers ' body . <p> http : **40;9293;TOOLONG ... To this day , Myrlie Evers is considered a high-profile US civil rights activist and her actions have been memorialised in history in fighting for her husband 's justice . <p> http : **40;9335;TOOLONG ... In 1869 , February 5 also marked the historic day that two miners found the world 's largest gold nugget , dubbed the Welcome Stranger , while prospecting in the gold fields of Victoria.The massive golden stone weighed 72kg and measured 61cm long when it was found buried just below the surface in the small town of Moliagul , around 200km north-west of Melbourne . <p> http : **40;9377;TOOLONG ... To this day the huge finds are on display at the National Museum in Canberra.As the story goes , miners John Deason and Richard Oates were the lucky pair to uncover the nugget after seven years of struggling to maintain a living . <p> http : **40;9419;TOOLONG ... In 1994 , February 5 was also the day of the tragic Markale market massacres carried out by the Army of Republika Srpska during the Siege of Sarajevo in the Bosnian War.Sixty-eight people were killed in total and 144 more were injured by a 120mm mortar . <p> http : **40;9461;TOOLONG ... The horrific scenes that came from within the marketplace after the attack were broadcast around the world and , at the time , it was claimed that the Bosnian army had shelled its own people to provoke intervention of Western countries on their side - however this is contested.That first attack was also followed up by a second shelling on August 28 , 1995 when five mortars killed 43 people and injured 75 others . <p> http : **40;9503;TOOLONG ... In 2001 , February 5 was the day that a spokesperson for Tom Cruise and Nicole Kidman announced they had officially separated after 12 years of marriage.At the time , the couple cited irreconcilable differences as the reasoning for their ultimate divorce , however former Church of Scientology executive Mark Rathbun later claimed in a documentary film that he was instructed to " facilitate Cruise 's break-up with Nicole Kidman " . <p> http : **40;9545;TOOLONG ... Patty Hearst , 19-year-old granddaughter of newspaper tycoon William Randolph Hearst , is kidnapped from her California home by a revolutionary group in 1974 . She was held in a closet for a week , blindfolded and with her hands tied , and forced to read political tracts . <p> http : **40;9587;TOOLONG ... The Symbionese Liberation Army ( SLA ) held Ms Hearst hostage , demanding the release from prison of two of their members.As a ransom , the group demanded her family distribute $70 to every needy Californian , more than they could afford . <p> http : **40;9629;TOOLONG ... After not being seen for two months , she and members of the SLA were seen robbing a bank . Hearst herself is seen clutching a rifle.In 1975 , she was arrested in an apartment with another SLA member and charged for the bank robbery . <p> http : **40;9671;TOOLONG ... Unconvinced she had been brainwashed , a judge sentenced her to 35 years in jail . <p> http : **40;9713;TOOLONG ... Her sentence was commuted by Jimmy Carter in 1979 , and pardoned by Bill Clinton in 2001 , on the last day of his presidency . <p> http : **40;9755;TOOLONG ... In 1977 , February 4 was the day of the Chicago Loop derailment disaster that was - at the time - the city 's worst-ever transit accident.In a bizarre set of circumstances , the incident saw four cars of a city train carriage launched off an escalated railway after jack-knifing before then plummeting to the ground below . <p> http : **40;9797;TOOLONG ... At about 5.25pm on February 4 , a Ravenswood train was waiting on the tracks , just past the northeast turn , waiting for another train to clear the platform of the State/Lake station.It was then that a third train began to move forward behind the Ravenswood service , against track and cab signals telling it to stop , making the two trains collide and begin to thrust up into the air off the tracks . <p> http : **40;9839;TOOLONG ... In the immense pressure caused by the collision , the first four cars of the Ravenswood train were launched off the tracks , while a fifth was left dangling in the air over the street below.A later investigation into the incident had revealed the driver responsible for the derailment had been under the influence of marijuana at the time.In total , 11 people were killed and more than 180 others injured in the disaster . <p> http : **40;9881;TOOLONG ... In 1976 , residents in the tiny Guatemalan town of Los Amates , north-east of Guatemala City , were woken in the early hours of the morning by a powerful earthquake that flattened entire buildings and shifted the country 's landscape.The 7.5-magnitude tremor first rocked the country at 03.01am on February 4 along the Motagua Fault and caused widespread damages . <p> http : **40;9923;TOOLONG ... The force of the earthquake saw entire houses simply fall away from their foundations after landslides were sparked in the chaos.With the earthquake also striking at such an early hour , many deaths were recorded because residents had been asleep when the damages began . <p> http : **40;9965;TOOLONG ... The most heavily affected area covered some 30,000 square kilometres , with a population of 2.5 million . Some 23,000 people were reported dead and 77,000 wounded.In rural areas , the force of the tremor was also so strong that rail tracks and infrastructure were physically moved or re-shaped in the quake . <p> http : **40;10007;TOOLONG ... Approximately 258,000 houses were destroyed on that day , leaving about 1.2 million people homeless . Forty per cent of Guatemala 's hospital infrastructure was also destroyed.In other areas closer to the fault line , the path of destruction could be tracked by the craters left along the ground . <p> http : **40;10049;TOOLONG ... In 2004 , the world 's largest and most prolific social network - Facebook - was officially launched by Mark Zuckerberg in a move that would re-shape the online world.The now-mega-famous website was initially launched as ' TheFacebook ' and was immediately the centre of a legal dispute involving Harvard seniors Cameron Winklevoss and Tyler Winklevoss who claimed Zuckerberg had stolen their idea . <p> http : **40;10091;TOOLONG ... While Facebook is now a global mega network , membership to the initial website was at first restricted to students of Harvard College.Within just one month , more than half the undergraduates at the college had registered . Within two months , it had expanded to Columbia , Stanford and Yale.Today , Facebook boasts more than 2.5 billion active users . <p> http : **40;10133;TOOLONG ... Melbourne-born murderer Ronald Ryan became the last person to be executed in Australia , on February 3 , 1967 . He was hung in Pentridge Prison , Melbourne . Ryan was given no special meal . <p> http : **40;10175;TOOLONG ... In 1606 , January 31 was the day famous English Catholic anarchist Guy Fawkes died prematurely during his execution over the failed Gunpowder Treason Plot of the year before.A former Spanish fighter , Fawkes had generated popularity for a Catholic revolt in England against then-King James I. <p> http : **40;11267;TOOLONG ... The plotters - a group of English Catholics - leased a cellar space beneath the UK House of Lords , with the plan to blow it up from underneath during the State Opening of Parliament on November 5 , 1605 . Fawkes was placed in charge of the gunpowder which they stockpiled in the cellar , however the plot was revealed in an anonymous letter that directed authorities via a search to him while he was guarding 36 barrels of gunpowder -- enough to reduce the House of Lords to rubble . <p> http : **40;11309;TOOLONG ... After his arrest , Fawkes was sentenced to death by execution , hanging and then to be traditionally drawn and quartered on January 31 , 1606 . Despite that , through a conscious effort or not , Fawkes fell from the scaffold where he was to be hanged and broke his neck , thus avoiding the agony of being killed by the English government . <p> http : **40;11351;TOOLONG ... The entire Guy Fawkes story has forever since been etched into history as synonymous with anarchism and rebellion against authorities and governments.In the modern day , that has embodied itself in the Guy Fawkes masks protesters are seen wearing around the world to hide their identities while also making a statement about their opposition to ruling powers . <p> http : **40;11393;TOOLONG ... In 1961 , January 31 was also the day NASA and " Ham the Astrochimp " made world history after he became the first chimp to be launched into space as part of the US ' Project Mercury.Ham 's vital signs and tasks her performed while in space were monitored by sensors and computers on Earth during the mission.Ham 's lever-pushing performance during his 16 minute , 39 second voyage was only a fraction of a second slower than his skills on Earth - demonstrating to scientists that tasks could be performed in space . <p> http : **40;11435;TOOLONG ... In 1865 , January 31 was the day slavery was officially abolished in the United States after the 13th Amendment to the country 's Constitution was passed.The measure was swiftly turned into law by nearly all Northern states , along with a sufficient number of border states , however it was with the approval of President Andrew Johnson that the Southern states of Alabama , North Carolina and Georgia also agreed and the amendment was fully adopted before the end of 1865 . <p> http : **40;11477;TOOLONG ... Though the amendment formally abolished slavery across the United States , it took some time yet for behaviours to change in the nation.Factors such as Black Codes , white supremacist violence , and selective enforcement of statutes meant some black Americans were forced to endure the torture associated with slavery for years to come . <p> http : **40;11519;TOOLONG ... On January 30 , 1969 in their final performance of their career as a united band - and perhaps one of the world 's most iconic gigs - The Beatles put on an unannounced concert atop the Apple Corps headquarters building in central London.Joined by keyboardist Billy Preston , the quartet played for 42 minutes before the UK Metropolitan Police asked them to reduce the volume . <p> http : **40;11561;TOOLONG ... It is widely accepted that the Beatles had been planning a return to live performances throughout the early sessions for their album ' Let It Be ' , but the idea for the rooftop concert had only been conceived just days before it happened.The band performed nine takes of five different songs as crowds of onlookers , many of whom were workers who happened to be on lunch breaks , gathered in the streets below and on the roofs of local buildings . <p> http : **40;11603;TOOLONG ... The concert famously ended with the conclusion of the song " Get Back " , and with John Lennon saying , " I 'd like to say thank you on behalf of the group and ourselves and I hope we 've passed the audition . " The end of that concert single-handedly ended an era of music for many fans . <p> http : **40;11645;TOOLONG ... While the group did record one more album , ' Abbey Road ' , by September 1969 they had unofficially separated.Many fans who witnessed the spectacle refused to believe it , claiming some of the songs performed on the roof showed the Beatles once again in top form . <p> http : **40;11687;TOOLONG ... January 30 also marks the day in 1933 that Nazi Germany leader Adolf Hitler was officially appointed as the nation 's Chancellor by President Paul von Hindenburg after a series of parliamentary elections.The Enabling Act , that adjoined Hitler 's rise to power , virtually assured that he could constitutionally exercise dictatorial power without legal objection . <p> http : **40;11729;TOOLONG ... After visiting Hindenburg to make the move official , crowds of supporter lined the streets and struggled with police as Hitler was driven away as the named leader of the country.From that point on , Hitler - being one of the Nazi Party 's best speakers , exercised his willingness to use violence in advancing his political objectives and to recruit party members who were willing to do the same . <p> http : **40;11771;TOOLONG ... Once in power , the Nazis created a mythology surrounding their rise to power that ultimately defined the second World War and the atrocity of the Final Solution . <p> http : **40;11813;TOOLONG ... In 1948 , January 30 was the date of Indian lawyer and anti-colonial nationalist Mahatma Gandhi 's assassination.Gandhi was killed in the compound of Birla House by Nathuram Godse - an advocate of Hindu nationalism , a member of the political party the Hindu Mahasabha and a past member of the Hindu nationalist paramilitary volunteer organisation Rashtriya Swayamsevak Sangh.The political icon was shot three times at point-blank range and , after a rushed trial , Godse was sentenced to death . <p> http : **40;11855;TOOLONG ... On January 29 , 1998 in what was believed to be the first-ever fatal bombing of an abortion clinic in the United States history , an off-duty police officer was violently killed and another nurse critically injured in an explosion in Birmingham that left the building in tatters.The New Woman , All Women Health Care Clinic , had long-been a target of pro-life protesters and the bomb was detonated as the facility opened in the morning . <p> http : **40;11897;TOOLONG ... Robert D. Sanderson , 35 , an eight-year police veteran who had been working part-time as a guard at the clinic and had been wearing plain clothes at the time was killed in the blast.Emily Lyons , a 41-year-old nurse who was opening the clinic , was also left fighting for her life . <p> http : **40;11939;TOOLONG ... The man later to be revealed as the killer bomber was Eric Robert Rudolph - a domestic terrorist who had already committed a series of anti-abortion and anti-gay-motivated bombings.After the attack , he remained on the run for five years before being captured in May 2003 while rummaging for food in a North Carolina Dumpster.In 2005 , he avoided the possibility of execution , agreeing to plead guilty in court to two earlier bombings . His plea agreement called for four life sentences without the possibility for parole and agreed to waive all appeals . <p> http : **40;11981;TOOLONG ... On January 29 in 2013 , another US tragedy occurred in Alabama - in Midland City.Jimmy Lee Dykes , a 65-year-old Vietnam War-era veteran , boarded a local school bus just after 3.30pm ( local time ) demanding that he could take two children , six and eight years old , both boys.The driver , 66-year-old Charles Albert Poland , Jr. , refused to allow him and was ultimately shot dead in a flurry of five bullets that left his lying in the vehicle 's aisle in front of horrified students . <p> http : **40;12023;TOOLONG ... Dykes then exited the bus along with five-year-old Midland City Elementary School student Ethan Gilman , who also has autism.The Vietnam veteran whisked the boy away to a secluded and underground war-style bunker on his property that contained homemade bombs and equipped with a PVC pipe coming out of the ground - which he later used to communicate with FBI agents . <p> http : **40;12065;TOOLONG ... Negotiations between Dykes and authorities spanned an entire week , while Ethan was still underground , before an elite FBI team used explosives and stun grenades to force entry into the bunker.Dykes was shot dead in the chaos that ensued and the young boy was safely rescued by police . <p> http : **40;12107;TOOLONG ... In the aftermath of the terrifying ordeal , Charles Albert Poland , Jr was heralded as an American hero for standing in the way of Dykes in an effort to protect the school children that had known him for years.Three weeks after the confrontation on Dykes ' property , the bunker was demolished by officials , who stated that it posed " a biological risk " . <p> http : **40;12149;TOOLONG ... In 1980 , January 29 was the day that Hungarian sculptor and professor of architecture Erno Rubik 's world-famous puzzle cube was officially licenced to be sold by the Ideal Toy Corp group.From that moment , the Rubik 's Cube has attracted global popularity and remains the world 's top-selling puzzle game.Since 2003 , the World Cube Association - the Rubik 's Cube 's international governing body - has also organised competitions worldwide for speed-solvers . <p> http : **40;12191;TOOLONG ... On January 28 , 2009 - 39 years after Queen Elizabeth II travelled around Australia on a royal tour with Prince Philip - an alleged plot to kill or seriously injure the monarch was revealed by a retired detective who had worked to save her life . In 1970 , the Queen had visited Australia in April and planned to visit multiple cities around the country . <p> http : **40;12233;TOOLONG ... During her travels across the nation , the detective said that the Queen resorted to long-distance trains to transport her from capital city to city as part of the tour.It was during one of these trips between Sydney and Orange in New South Wales , however , that the detective said she narrowly avoided disaster . <p> http : **40;12275;TOOLONG ... On the trip , the detective said that a large log had been placed along the train tracks ahead of the Queen 's carriage in the hope it would derail the vehicle while the monarch was on-board.The alleged assassination plot ultimately failed because , at the time , the train had been travelling slowly when it struck the log , despite still sliding for 700 metres before coming to a stop . The royal couple were unaware of the attempt until 2009 when the incident was first reported . <p> http : **40;12317;TOOLONG ... On January 24 , 1989 America 's notorious serial killer , kidnapper and rapist Ted Bundy was finally executed via the electric chair at at Florida State Prison following a life of atrocities committed against young women and then a decade of denials.Before his execution he confessed to 30 homicides that he committed in seven different states between 1974 and 1978 . Despite that , the true number of victims is unknown and possibly higher . <p> http : **40;13451;TOOLONG ... Throughout his time in the media spotlight and the courtroom , Bundy was regarded as a handsome and charismatic person - which he exploited to win over his young victims . It was widely believed that he would approach his victims in public places , misguiding them using a false injury or impairment before knocking them unconscious , taking them to secluded locations and then raping and killing them.For years Bundy was not connected to a string of killings in different states , however as his guise began to unfold , a search of his prized Volkswagen car uncovered the items pictured here . <p> http : **40;13493;TOOLONG ... In 1975 , Bundy was first jailed for aggravated kidnapping and attempted criminal assault . He then became a suspect in a progressively longer list of unsolved homicides in several states around the USA.Despite two successful jail escapes , captures and more crimes against women he was eventually sentenced to death - to the cheers of people who lined Florida State Prison to await his execution . <p> http : **40;13535;TOOLONG ... In 1995 , the high-profile OJ Simpson murder trial officially began in a Los Angeles court with the opening prosecution statement provided by Deputy District Attorney Christopher Darden.A former NFL star , Simpson had been charged on two counts of murder for the June 12 , 1994 slashing deaths of his ex-wife , Nicole Brown Simpson , and her friend Ron Goldman . <p> http : **40;13577;TOOLONG ... In his opening statement , Darden directly addressed the jury by stating that , while they may have seen the public persona of Simpson on television broadcasts and NFL adverts , there was another private side to Simpson that the trial would reveal . " The evidence will show that the face you see , and the man you will see , is the face of a batterer , a wife beater , an abuser , a controller , " he told the court " " You will see the face of Ron and Nicole 's murderer . " The trial spanned an impressive 10 months and an extensive range of testimonies and evidence , however Simpson was ultimately acquitted . <p> http : **40;13619;TOOLONG ... In 1939 , January 24 also marked the day of the most devastating earthquake in Chile 's history when the small coastal town of Chillan was rocked by an 8.3-magnitude tremor that flattened the entire destination and killed up to 28,000 people.Within a period of three minutes both Chillan and the nearby city of Concepcion had been struck by the initial quake and multiple aftershocks that caused widespread destruction , public panic and buildings collapse onto residents.The disaster sparked the establishment of a government department that revolutionised Chile 's response to earthquakes and , to this day , the Chillan incident is marked as one of the country 's worst-ever tremors . <p> http : **40;13661;TOOLONG ... In 1957 , January 23 was the day that marked the birth of what is now famously known as the " Frisbee " . US inventor Walter Morrison first created the children 's toy in 1946 under the name , the " Whirlo-Way " . By 1955 , after a series of failed sales attempts and re-designs Morrison had renamed it the Pluto Platter before the Wham-O toy company bought the rights for the toy on January 23 - which was also the inventor 's birthday.Further to that , it took Wham-O another two years to give the toy the name that made it world-famous after learning college students had been calling it that . <p> http : **40;13703;TOOLONG ... On January 3 , 1986 - three years after its establishment , the Rock and Roll Hall of Fame inducted its first-ever recipients from a mega class of talent that spanned decades prior to the creation of the museum and foundation.One of the biggest names to join the first ranks of the Hall of Fame was that of Rock and Roll legend Elvis Presley , who had died nine years prior to his induction.Regarded as one of the most significant cultural icons of the 20th century , Elvis gained the epithet ' The King of Rock and Roll ' throughout his career and he currently remains the best-selling solo music artist of all time . <p> http : **40;13745;TOOLONG ... Joining Presley was funk music icon - the " Godfather of Soul " , James Brown.The Rock and Roll Hall of Fame inducts artists into its ranks based on the merit of their careers and the influence they bring to the genre and to the music industry.When it came to James ' induction , the former gospel singer was regarded as one of the greatest artists of all time and to this day is still known for the contribution of his songs to social commentary along race lines . <p> http : **40;13787;TOOLONG ... One of the other major Hall of Fame inductees on January 23 was mid-1950s American musician and singer-songwriter Buddy Holly.Holly was known to have opened for Elvis Presley multiple times before launching his own stellar musical career in which he is regarded to have defined the traditional rock-and-roll lineup of two guitars , bass , and drums . <p> http : **40;13829;TOOLONG ... In 1937 , January 23 was the day of the second instalment of what came to be known as the Moscow Trials in Soviet Russia.The trials were a series of court hearings that were aimed at eliminating Soviet opposition.In this trial , 17 figureheads were put before a court accused of being anti-Soviet Trotskyists.Of those 17 , thirteen of the defendants were eventually executed by shooting after the trial , while the rest received sentences in labour camps . <p> http : **40;13871;TOOLONG ... The Moscow Trials are historically regarded as part of Joseph Stalin 's ( right ) " Great Purge " of his political opponents who were accused of representing the growing discontent among his ranks.Soviety Russia under Stalin at the time had led to an economic and political crisis that had damaged his reputation and - while being conscious of this - he took steps to prevent a backlash against his increasingly totalitarian rule . <p> http : **40;13913;TOOLONG ... January 22 marked the day in 1957 that the American ' Mad Bomber ' , George Metesky was arrested for his role in 16 years of continuous bombings of theatres , terminals , libraries , and offices that injured as many as 15 people.An investigation into his behaviour later revealed that Metesky had grown angry and resentful during his life about events surrounding a workplace injury suffered years prior to his arrest.Metesky is known to have planted at least 33 bombs , of which 22 exploded . <p> http : **40;13955;TOOLONG ... Metesky often placed warning calls to the buildings where he had planted bombs , but would not specify the device 's exact location.Generally , they were gunpowder-filled pipe bombs , ranging in size and most used timers constructed from flashlight batteries and pocket watches . Metesky 's arrest ultimately came as a result of an early form of New York offender profiling , using clues given in letters he wrote to a newspaper.He was found legally insane and committed to a state mental hospital before he died on May 23 , 1994 aged 90 . <p> http : **40;13997;TOOLONG ... On January 22 , 2008 aged just 28 , Australian film icon Heath Ledger died as a result of a drug overdose in his apartment in New York City.Born in Perth , the young actor 's career spanned 19 different feature films - including hit titles such as ' 10 Things I Hate About You ' , ' A Knight 's Tale ' and ' Brokeback Mountain ' - and he had begun to develop a global fan following . <p> http : **40;14039;TOOLONG ... His skill on-camera drew widespread respect and praise from international directors who would often applaud his ability to explore his talent in the films he starred in while also approaching each character he was assigned in a multitude of different manners.Despite that praise , Ledger always spoke of how he disconnected himself from perfectionism , instead saying : " I 'm always gon na pull myself apart and dissect the work . " <p> http : **40;14081;TOOLONG ... Ledger 's final major role in Hollywood was that of The Joker in Christopher Nolan 's ' The Dark Knight ' as part of the Batman franchise.At the time of his death , the film had already entered the editing stage and some were quick to question whether the role had negatively affected his mental health.Despite that , his performance earned him universal acclaim and popularity from fans and critics , as well as numerous posthumous awards including the Academy Award for Best Supporting Actor . <p> http : **40;14123;TOOLONG ... Ledger 's New York apartment was also swamped in the days after his death with tributes , flowers , gifts and honours placed by his fans who visited the scene.His passing sparked a nationwide outpouring of grief in Australia and many actors , politicians , musicians and institutions paid their tributes to him or dedicated awards , buildings or creations to him . <p> http : **40;14165;TOOLONG ... In 1984 , the world of computing changed forever on January 22 with the world introduction of the Macintosh computer in a television advert that aired during the third quarter of Super Bowl XVIII.The ad , titled " 1984 " , used an unnamed heroine to represent the coming of the Macintosh as a means of saving humanity from IBM 's attempts to dominate the computer industry.Two days later , the computer went on sale and by the end of the year purchases had boomed so much that Apple had expected to sell at least 250,000 units.That moment in global technology also spurred an entire line of Apple computers that have since revolutionised the industry . <p> http : **40;14207;TOOLONG ... In 1976 , the world 's first-ever supersonic passenger plane - the British and French , turbojet-powered Concorde - was introduced to the world for travel on January 21 . The aircraft had a maximum speed over twice the speed of sound at 2180km/h at cruise altitude and provided seating for up to 128 passengers.The Concorde was jointly developed and manufactured by Sud Aviation and the British Aircraft Corporation and was used mainly by wealthy passengers who could afford to pay a high price . <p> http : **40;14249;TOOLONG ... The Concorde flew regular transatlantic flights between London 's Heathrow Airport and Paris 's Charles de Gaulle Airport to John F. Kennedy International Airport in New York CIty , Washington Dulles International Airport in Virginia , and Grantley Adams International Airport in Barbados.It also flew these routes in less than half the time of other airliners . The aircraft was in operation for 27 years until 2003 when it was discontinued and in all that time there was just one fatal crash - that of Air France Flight 4590 on July 25 , 2000That plane erupted into flames after departing from Charles de Gaulle Airport , killing all 100 passengers and nine crew members on board and four people on the ground . <p> http : **40;14291;TOOLONG ... In 1968 , January 21 was also the date one of the most widely-publicised and controversial battles of the Vietnam War began - the Battle of Khe Sanh.In what was a forward push by the North Vietnamese People 's Army of Vietnam , US troops stationed at the Khe Sanh Combat Base , south of Hanoi , came under heavy fire after being surrounded.That push led to five months of intense combat that included artillery , mortar , and rocket attacks . <p> http : **40;14333;TOOLONG ... In response to the siege , the US Air Force launched an operation that saw 100,000 tons of bombs dropped and over 158,000 artillery rounds fired.That equated to 5000 bombs being dropped daily , exploding the same amount of land as five Hiroshima-sized atomic bombs . <p> http : **40;14375;TOOLONG ... The intense combat only came to an end on July 9 after a mission code-named Operation Pegasus that saw an influx of US military men come to the assistance of the surrounded troops.That move saw the US attempt to claim a victory in the battle , despite the Vietnamese also doing the same for having driven them out of the area.In total , just 274 US troops were killed in the battle and thousands more injured while up to eight-times more casualties than that were recorded for the Vietnamese Army . <p> http : **40;14417;TOOLONG ... In 1981 , January 21 was the day the now-world-famous DMC DeLorean sports car first came into mass production for consumers , priced at US$25,000 ( equivalent to US$70,000 or AUD$101,000 in 2019 ) . The car , designed by Giorgetto Giugiaro , first gained popularity for its strange design and gull-wing doors , however it quickly became notorious for its lack of power and performance , which did not match consumers ' expectations . <p> http : **40;14459;TOOLONG ... Despite that disappointing beginning however , the Delorean was to become more popular than ever with the 1985 release of the ' Back to the Future ' film franchise that made the vehicle iconic in popular culture as a time machine.Those films saw the vehicle - which had only initially been in production for two years - created and re-created thousands of times by fans and collectors with differing modifications.It is believed that as many as 6500 Deloreans are still in existence around the world . <p> http : **40;14501;TOOLONG ...
